金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 05 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國製造業維持擴張，成本壓力升高：9 月 ISM 製造業調查顯示，美國製造業連續第九個月
位於擴張區間，新訂單與就業分項均見改善，反映接單與用人需求仍有支撐。然而，價格指數
由 8 月的 71.1 升至 9 月的 77.9，能源、金屬及關稅相關成本上升，加上供應商交貨偏慢，企
業面臨更大成本壓力 。 後續須觀察企業能否轉嫁成本 ， 以及通膨黏性對聯準會政策判斷的影響 。
◼ 日本短觀顯示製造業改善、服務業略緩：日本銀行 10 月 1 日公布的 9 月短觀顯示，大型製造

業景氣判斷指數由 6 月的＋22 升至＋24，大型非製造業則由＋37 降至＋35。企業預估 12 月
兩項指數分別回落至＋21 與＋30，顯示現況仍有支撐，後續看法則偏審慎。製造業改善有助
支撐日股獲利預期，但服務業放緩及日銀政策路徑仍須持續觀察。
◼ 台灣製造業活動加速擴張，AI 需求續強：10 月 1 日公布的製造業調查顯示，台灣 9 月製造業
活動擴張速度創 2021 年 8 月以來最快，AI 相關應用需求仍是重要推力。國發會 9 月 29 日公
布的 8 月景氣對策信號續呈紅燈，綜合判斷分數為 41 分，與 7 月持平，領先及同時指標皆續
升。科技供應鏈景氣仍有支撐，但成長動能能否擴及其他產業，仍是觀察後續景氣的重點。
市場展望
◼ AI 需求持續擴張，關注財報季表現： AI 基礎建設需求未受利率環境影響，而是持續擴張，帶
動光通訊、CCL、ABF 載板 、記憶體、先進製程與封裝等關鍵供應鏈維持強勁成長，市場表
現由全面上漲轉向結構性分化。隨新一代 AI 伺服器放量、CSP 資本支出維持高檔，具供給優

勢、定價能力及訂單能見度延伸至 2027 年的龍頭廠商仍可望受惠。10 月中旬起陸續公布的
第三季財報，若結果持續表現亮眼，可望為市場再添動能，屆時具 AI 供應鏈題材的台股與日
股亦可望受惠。
◼ 債券佈局聚焦 5~10 年期美國公債及投等債：雖然貨幣政策風險 下降有助於穩定長期通膨預
期，但政府與企業發債供給增加所帶來的排擠效應，以及美國經濟成長展現韌性，仍可能使長
天期利率面臨上行壓力。因此，在當前利率環境下，5~10 年期債券較能兼顧票息收益與存續
期間風險管理。投資級債方面，彭博美國投資級公司債指數利差約 80-90 個基點，雖然利差
偏窄，但高基準利率環境仍提供具吸引力的絕對收益水準，因此現階段5~10 年期美國公債及
投資級債的風險報酬結構相對較佳。

利率上行引發波動，惟無損 AI 供應鏈擴張動能
2026 年 10 月
9 月全球股市普遍回落，主因為油價急升、全球長端殖利率攀高及主要央行轉鷹。僅有日本股市
受 AI、半導體需求與企業信心支撐，抵銷 BOJ 升息壓力，成為主要市場中唯一正報酬。標普 500 9
月小跌 0.3%，雖 AI 與大型科技股獲利韌性仍在，但 Fed 於 9 月升息並暗示年底前再度升息，加上
油價與美債殖利率走高，壓抑估值並引發科技股震盪。印度股市下跌 6.9%最弱，主要受油價飆升、
全球利率上行及外資單月淨流出約 27 億美元拖累。中國股市下跌 4.7%，因受內需修復偏弱、科技

股評價承壓所致；歐洲股市也因油價單月大漲近 14%與全球殖利率攀升影響，9 月下跌 4.6%。
9 月主要債市全面下跌，核心壓力來自全球殖利率急升。美歐日央行同步升息、Brent 原油單月
上漲約 14%、 美國經濟數據偏強與公債供給疑慮 ， 使美國10 年期殖利率單月上升約 53bp，創 2022
年以來最大升幅。主要券種中，全球投資級公司債下跌 2.93%表現最弱，新興美元債亦下跌 2.91%，
因 9 月全球公債殖利率快速攀升，此兩類債券對利率變化均較敏感 ； 其中投資級公司債相較高收益債
存續期較長、票息緩衝較低，因此在利率重新定價期間承受較大價格壓力。亞洲高收益債下挫 1.20%
相對抗跌，因高票息與較短存續期提供緩衝；新興當地債跌 1.74%，亦優於多數美元債種。
在股市方面，AI 基礎建設需求持續擴張，帶動 HBM、伺服器 DRAM、先進製程與封裝等關鍵供
應鏈維持成長動能，市場表現由全面上漲逐步轉向結構性分化。若AI 伺服器需求持續成長且 CSP 資
本支出維持高檔，具供給優勢、定價能力及較高訂單能見度之企業，其營運表現有機會獲得支撐。整

體而言 ， 科技產業與台灣及日本股市獲利表現仍具關注度 ， 惟後續仍須留意全球景氣 、 產業供需變化 、
政策監管及市場評價調整等風險因素。
就債市而言，雖然貨幣政策風險下降有助於穩定長期通膨預期，但政府與企業發債供給增加所帶
來的排擠效應，以及美國經濟成長展現韌性，仍可能使長天期利率面臨上行壓力。因此，在當前利率
環境下，中天期債券於票息收益與存續期間風險之間相對較為均衡。投資級債方面，彭博美國投資級
公司債指數利差約 80 個基點 ， 雖然利差偏窄 ， 但高基準利率環境仍提供相對較高的收益水準 。 因此，
在目前市場環境下 ， 中天期美國公債及投資級債仍為值得持續觀察的債券資產類別 ， 惟後續表現仍須
留意利率走勢、信用利差變化及經濟成長動能等因素。

美國股市：通膨與升息預期上升推動美債利率上漲，美股呈高檔區間震盪
9 月因美伊關係緊張升溫 ， 推升油價並加劇市場對通膨回升的擔憂 ；相較於 6 月點陣圖 ，9 月FOMC
官員全面上修 2026 至 2027 年的政策利率預估至 4.1%，並暗示 2026 年底前會再升息一次，加上美
國經濟數據表現優於預期，使美國公債殖利率走升，進而壓抑美股表現。在此背景下，9 月標普 500
指數呈現高檔區間震盪格局。產業表現方面，資訊科技與通訊服務類股相對強勢，持續扮演指數上漲
的主要動能；相較之下，醫療保健、必需消費及部分工業類股表現較為疲弱。

9 月美國聯準會貨幣政策會議以 12 比 0 的表決結果 ， 將聯邦基金利率目標區間上調25 個基點至
3.75%到 4.00%。9 月最新經濟預測，美國聯準會官員上修 2026 年核心 PCE 至 3.4%，2027 年維持
在 2.5%，將 2026-2027 年經濟成長上調 0.1%至 2.3%與 2.4%，顯示聯準會認為美國經濟與通膨仍
具韌性。
觀察美國近期的經濟數據可以發現企業投資動能依然強健，元大投顧表示，美國8 月扣除飛機與
國防的核心資本財訂單由前期的月增 0.6%加速至 1.6%， 其中初級金屬 、 機械及電機設備訂單均呈現
增長，顯示 AI 相關投資及企業設備更新需求持續支撐資本財訂單，企業設備投資可望對經濟成長形
成穩健貢獻。
自 6 月底以來 ， 市場持續上修標普500 指數企業獲利預估 ， 其中以科技與能源類股貢獻最為明顯 ，
反映企業營運表現優於市場預期。AI 投資循環持續推進、企業數位轉型需求增加，以及美國內需維
持穩健，共同支撐企業獲利成長動能，並有助於吸收高利率環境帶來的壓力。與此同時，標普 500

指數前瞻本益比已由先前高點回落，評價壓力較先前明顯緩解。隨著企業獲利持續改善，美股行情正
由過去仰賴資金與情緒推升評價 ， 逐步轉向由基本面支撐股價 ， 後續表現將更取決於企業獲利是否持
續優於預期。
投資策略方面，投資焦點已逐漸由評價擴張轉向企業獲利成長與基本面表現。短期市場仍須留意
政策面不確定性 ， 美中貿易談判進展將牽動全球供應鏈與科技產業展望 ， 若關稅或科技限制措施再度
升溫，可能對科技股評價造成壓力；此外，美國期中選舉逐漸接近，企業稅率、科技監管及產業政策
等議題亦可能影響市場風險偏好。整體而言，企業獲利改善仍是支撐美股最重要的力量，若 AI 投資
循環與獲利成長趨勢延續，美股中長期表現仍可望由基本面驅動。

歐洲股市：儘管企業獲利上修，仍需持續關注地緣政治發展
9 月泛歐 600 指數下跌約 2%，產業表現方面 ，製藥 、生技、銀行及部分科技類股表現相對強勢，
顯示資金偏好具獲利能見度與防禦特性的族群 ； 相較之下 ， 礦業 、 消費及部分金融服務類股表現落後 ，
反映市場對歐洲經濟成長及企業獲利前景仍持審慎態度。整體而言，受到高油價以及高利率環境，投
資氣氛轉趨保守，市場資金持續聚焦於基本面較穩健的產業。
歐洲央行 9 月政策利率由 2.25% 升息 1 碼至 2.50 % ，符合市場預期。歐洲央行最新經濟預
測中上修中期通膨展望，2027 年消費者物價指數年增率由 2.3% 升至 2.5% ，2028 年則由 2.0%

上調至 2.1% ；核心消費者物價指數方面，2027 年和 2028 年預測亦各上修 0.1 % 至 2.6% 及
2.3% ，能源成本上升對食品及其他價格的傳導，使得通膨回落速度較原先預期緩慢。ECB 總裁拉加
德表示能源價格上漲逐步傳導至核心及食品通膨，預期整體通膨在 2027 年上半年維持在高檔。
根據花旗歐洲獲利修正指數(Earnings Revision Index) ，歐洲企業獲利預估已連續上修並到近五
年高點，反映企業基本面持續改善。受惠於歐元區景氣穩步復甦、製造業景氣回升，以及企業資本支
出逐步回暖，工業、金融及景氣循環類股獲利展望同步改善。隨著市場對企業未來盈餘預期提高，有
助支撐歐股評價面與資金流入，並成為推動泛歐 600 指數表現的重要動能。
然而，自 3 月美伊衝突爆發以來，市場對能源供給中斷的擔憂持續升溫，帶動油價走高，進而增
加仰賴進口能源的歐洲企業成本壓力，並壓抑歐股相對表現。進入9 月後，俄羅斯與北約緊張關係再
度升溫，歐洲安全局勢不確定性提高，進一步加劇市場對能源供應與地緣政治風險的擔憂。若相關衝

突持續延燒，不僅可能推升能源價格與通膨壓力，也可能影響企業投資意願及市場風險偏好，進而對
歐洲企業獲利與股市表現形成壓力。
展望 10 月，歐股仍將受到高油價、地緣政治風險及歐洲央行維持偏鷹立場等因素影響，市場波
動性可能維持偏高。近期能源價格攀升推升通膨預期，使 ECB 傾向維持較高利率水準，不僅提高企
業融資成本，也限制股市評價進一步擴張的空間。此外，中東局勢持續緊張，以及俄羅斯與北約間的
緊張關係再度升高，均可能影響投資人風險偏好，增添歐洲金融市場不確定性。雖然歐元區經濟活動
仍維持溫和擴張，企業獲利預估亦持續改善，但在高利率與地緣政治風險尚未明顯緩解之前，市場情
緒仍偏向審慎，後續仍需持續關注能源價格、通膨走勢及地緣政治發展，作為判斷歐股能否重啟上漲
動能的重要觀察指標。

日本股市：升息落地，景氣展望佳，第四季半導體與商社企業獲利動能有望延續
9 月日本股市受升息預期及長天期公債殖利率走高影響，部分高評價股票出現震盪，但這波調整
較偏向政策轉折下的評價重估，而非企業基本面全面轉弱。日本銀行於 9 月 18 日決議將短期政策利
率目標提高至約 1.25%，使市場反覆討論的升息方向逐漸明朗。隨著政策落地，投資焦點有望由利率
變動轉回企業營運與獲利。從正面角度看，利率正常化反映日本經濟具備一定韌性，股價整理也有助
於消化先前累積的評價壓力。若後續企業財測保持穩健，9 月的震盪可望成為第四季行情重新蓄勢的

過程。
日本股市的中期支撐，來自薪資成長、內需改善與企業投資的相互帶動。2026 年春鬥最終統計
顯示，5,368 個工會的平均調薪幅度達 5.01%，連續第三年維持在 5%以上；可明確辨識的基本薪資
調升幅度亦達 3.50%。薪資持續增加，有助於提升家庭消費能力，為零售與服務等內需產業提供較穩
定的需求基礎。若企業能透過產品競爭力、定價能力及經營效率吸收成本，消費回溫便有機會轉化為
獲利成長。同時，全球 AI 相關投資也為日本半導體設備、載板及電子零組件供應鏈創造機會，使內
需復甦與科技需求形成雙重支撐。
展望 10 月，隨著升息議題逐步由預期轉為已知條件，市場可望更加重視第四季訂單、企業財測
及實際獲利。科技產業方面，若全球 AI 相關資本支出持續擴大，日本具技術優勢的設備與零組件企
業，有機會將需求轉化為接單及出貨成長；商社與資源相關企業，則可觀察能源、原物料業務及投資
收益對現金流的貢獻。內需產業亦值得留意薪資成長能否帶動消費，以及企業能否維持利潤率。整體
而言，10 月的投資機會不必侷限於單一題材，市場有望逐步從政策消息主導，轉向由產業需求與企

業業績支撐的行情。
投資策略方面，半導體供應鏈可優先挑選訂單能見度高、具技術門檻且獲利成長明確的企業；商
社則著重現金流、資本配置與股東回饋，內需股則觀察消費回溫能否反映在營收及利潤。若股價因利
率或匯率波動而短暫回落，但企業基本面與財測未明顯改變，可考慮分批布局，避免追逐短線漲幅。
後續仍須追蹤長天期利率 、 日圓及企業財測變化 ， 但投資主軸可更積極地放在具備實質成長能力的公
司。日本央行升息落地後，企業獲利能否接棒，將是日本股市延續表現的關鍵。

台灣股市：訂單持續暢旺突破千億，AI 需求持續向供應鏈擴散，廠商展望樂觀
9 月觀察台股基本面，最明確的支撐來自 8 月外銷訂單創下單月新高，以及企業對 9 月接單維持
擴張預期。經濟部公布，8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6 億美元，月增 5.1%、年增 71.4%；其中資訊通信
與電子產品合計 799.6 億美元，占整體 77.7%，顯示 AI 伺服器、半導體與雲端建設仍是主要成長來
源。受查廠商對 9 月接單的預期，以訂單金額計算的動向指數為 52.1，高於代表持平的 50，意味整
體接單有望較 8 月增加。雖然 9 月實際結果仍待公布，但已發布的數據與企業展望，為台股進入第

四季提供了正面的基本面依據。
更值得留意的是，AI 需求正從核心晶片延伸至更廣的供應鏈。雲端業者擴充運算能力，除了帶動
伺服器與半導體接單，也使自研 ASIC、IC 設計及相關電子零組件成為後續觀察重點；半導體投資與
設備擴產 ， 則為自動化設備 、 銅箔及高階材料創造機會 。8 月機械與基本金屬外銷訂單分別年增 28.3%
及 26.8%，顯示科技投資的效益已反映在部分非電子貨品。對台股而言，這有助於投資主線從少數大
型權值股，延伸至具技術優勢與訂單能見度的中上游企業。不過，面板需求仍偏弱，產業表現並非全
面同步，選股仍須辨別企業實際受惠程度。
展望 10 月，市場焦點可望轉向第三季財報、第四季營運展望，以及 9 月外銷訂單能否驗證接單
續強。就廠商對 9 月的預期而言，資訊通信與電子產品的訂單金額動向指數分別為 52.4 及 51.5，顯
示科技需求仍具支撐；基本金屬與化學品則達 59.0 及 61.0，呈現部分材料領域接單改善的期待。若
AI 伺服器出貨 、 自研晶片開發及半導體資本支出持續推進 ， 相關台灣供應鏈的營收與獲利有望受惠。

另一方面，機械與光學器材指數分別為 46.3 及 49.5，提醒市場不能將整體訂單成長視為所有產業同
步復甦，仍須以後續實績檢驗。
投資策略方面，10 月以「掌握 AI 主線、尋找擴散機會、重視獲利驗證」作為配置方向。核心部
位可留意伺服器、半導體及 IC 設計等需求較明確的企業，並觀察 ASIC 相關業務能否由開發逐步轉
化為穩定出貨；其他機會則可從設備、銅箔與高階材料中，篩選確實取得訂單的公司。選股不只看產
業接單增幅，更應比較個別企業的營收成長、毛利率與獲利展望，避免在評價已高時追逐短期題材。
整體而言，台股仍有基本面支撐，後續行情能否擴大，關鍵在於 AI 需求能否持續轉化為更多企業的
實際獲利。

中國股市：AI 產業規模擴張難掩獲利困境，產業進入變現考驗
9 月中國 AI 產業的討論，從模型能力與使用量，逐漸轉向更根本的問題：在高階晶片取得受限、
業者競爭激烈的環境下，龐大的投入能否形成可持續的回報。9 月中美高層會談後，雙方宣布建立
AI 相關對話機制，但公開成果未顯示高階 AI 晶片出口限制已放寬。政策溝通有所進展，並未立即改
變企業取得先進算力的條件 。 與此同時 ， 低價及開放權重策略持續降低AI 使用門檻 ， 帶動市場擴張，
也使企業更難單靠模型服務取得理想利潤。對投資人而言，9 月最重要的訊號不是單一產品的表現，

而是中國 AI 已進入需要同時回答算力效率、商業變現與資本回收的階段。
從整體結構來看，中國具備電力、製造基礎及廣泛應用場景，有利於 AI 以較低成本進入企業營
運與日常服務；但部署成本較低，不代表產業獲利便能同步提高。當業者以低價爭取用戶，使用量可
以迅速累積，收入卻可能跟不上基礎設施、研發及營運支出。以特定平台的觀察為例，DeepSeek 曾
取得可觀的代幣使用量，但相應支出占比明顯較低；這不能代表中國 AI 產業的整體財務表現，卻凸
顯「有人使用」與「願意付費」之間仍有距離。未來產業能否改善獲利，取決於企業是否找到具持續
需求的應用、建立合理收費方式，並避免同質化服務陷入反覆降價。
展望 10 月 ， 中國AI 的成長空間仍在 ， 但市場衡量進展的標準應由使用規模 ， 逐步轉向投入產出。
若 AI 更深入企業工作流程，相關需求有望延續；然而，需求增加後，業者仍須證明服務品質、運算
效率與客戶付費能同步改善 。 晶片限制使算力使用效率更加重要 ， 價格競爭則使收入品質成為另一項
考驗。Vercel 資料顯示，8 月開放權重模型在其平台占代幣使用量 56%，支出占比卻為 14%，說明

高使用量未必對應高支出；10 月宜觀察企業是否開始披露更清楚的付費需求與成本改善，而非只比
較流量或模型排名。
投資策略方面，對中國 AI 宜維持關注成長、審慎評估獲利的態度，避免把產業滲透率提高，直
接等同於所有企業的投資價值提升。評估重點可放在三個整體問題：第一，持續增加的算力與研發投
入，能否換來足夠的收入；第二，低價競爭下，企業能否留住付費客戶並維持合理利潤；第三，AI
應用能否從試用走向長期採購。具備穩定需求、成本控制能力及清楚收費模式的企業，較有機會在產
業整合中建立優勢。整體而言，中國 AI 並非缺少使用場景，而是仍須證明大規模使用可以轉化為可
持續的獲利，這將是 10 月觀察產業與相關市場表現的核心。

科技類股：AI 應用加速落地，光通訊與 Agent 入口迎來成長機會
AI 產業的成長主軸正由模型訓練延伸至推論與實際應用 ， 讓軟體服務與硬體建設形成相互帶動的
循環。當 AI 不再只回答問題，而能協助搜尋資訊、安排活動及完成跨平台任務，使用頻率與運算需
求有望提高。Meta 於 9 月推出個人 AI 助理 Muse，透過應用程式及 WhatsApp 接觸既有用戶，積
極爭取 AI 服務入口。對產業而言，Agent 普及不僅意味新的服務形式，也可能擴大雲端運算與資料
傳輸需求，使應用端的創新成為基礎設施升級的推力。投資機會可從使用者入口延伸至相關供應鏈。

在硬體端，AI 推論與多步驟任務若持續增加，資料中心內部的資料交換將更頻繁，高速且節能的
連接能力因而更具價值 。 光通訊除支援資料中心之間的傳輸 ， 也逐步朝機櫃及晶片附近的互連場景發
展 ， 拓展供應商的產品空間 。Lumentum 指出 ，AI 運算對速度與頻寬的要求提升 ， 帶動光連結需求，
並已在營收展望中反映相關成長 。 高速光模組與新型互連方案也持續推進 。 雖然部分新技術仍須經過
客戶導入與量產驗證，但既有產品需求與後續技術升級有望接續，讓光通訊成為 AI 基礎設施中值得
持續關注的環節。
在應用端，Muse 代表 AI 從被動回覆走向主動協助執行任務的嘗試。若 Agent 能替使用者完成
搜尋、比較、預訂與付款等流程，便利性提升有望促進使用黏著度，並為支付、交易與服務串接平台
創造新的合作機會。跨平台操作可能由單一入口整合，促使各產業重新思考客戶服務。 「Muse 對各
產業的影響」圖可呈現商業模式轉變的可能方向。隨著更多服務接入 Agent 生態系，使用情境有望
持續擴展；後續若任務完成率、使用留存及付費轉換逐步改善，應用端便有機會形成更明確的收入來

源，並進一步支持運算與傳輸投資。
投資策略方面，建議以「應用入口與基礎設施並重」掌握 AI 從訓練走向推論的成長機會。硬體
方面，可優先關注已切入主要客戶供應鏈、具備高速光模組與新型互連產品布局，且訂單能見度較佳
的業者。應用方面，則可追蹤具備用戶基礎、服務整合能力與清楚收費模式的平台，觀察 Agent 能
否從新產品熱度轉化為持續使用與付費需求 。 配置上可先以業績較明確的基礎設施業者為核心 ， 再逐
步增加應用平台的布局，兼顧當前需求與後續成長空間。整體而言，AI 應用愈深入日常生活與企業
流程 ， 入口平台與光通訊供應鏈愈有機會相互受惠 ； 選股關鍵仍是找出能將需求成長轉化為實際業績
的企業。

拉美市場：10 月巴西總統大選將登場，政治不確定性影響市場表現
9 月拉美市場維持震盪整理 。 一方面 ， 拉美各國通膨數據普遍低於市場預期 ，有利市場降息預期；
另一方面，巴西總統大選選情持續拉鋸，政治不確定性升溫則讓投資人保持觀望。展望 10 月，隨巴
西大選投票日逼近，政治因素仍可能使拉美市場短線波動加劇。
受到美伊衝突帶動油價上漲影響，市場原本擔心燃料與運輸成本增加，會讓拉美物價再度大幅上
漲，進而迫使拉美央行升息。然而，高盛分析指出，拉美市場目前的通膨率仍受到控制，當地經濟承
受能源漲價的能力 ， 比市場原先擔心的情況更好 。 其中一個重要原因是 ， 新興市場政府透過燃油補貼 、

稅制安排與零售價格管制，讓消費者實際感受到的價格漲幅相對有限。此外，上半年全球農產品供給
較為充裕，使食品價格漲幅偏低，也抵銷了部分能源漲價帶來的影響。由於拉美市場實際通膨壓力小
於原先市場預期，央行進一步大幅升息的必要性也隨之降低，高盛因此認為，市場目前對多數拉美經
濟體未來一至兩年的升息預期過於悲觀，並預期拉美各國央行在 2027 年較可能重新轉向降息。
不過，拉美多國經濟出現放緩跡象，亦可能壓抑股市上漲空間。智利央行於 9 月會議維持政策利
率 4.50%不變 ， 並將2026 年經濟成長預測下修至 0.25%至 0.75%， 低於6 月預估的 1.0%至 1.75%。
智利央行指出，第二季內需成長放緩，勞動市場及信心指標同步轉弱，加上燃料價格上漲侵蝕企業與
家庭所得，均對經濟活動形成壓力。巴西經濟亦出現放緩跡象，巴西央行於 9 月 16 日一致決議調降
Selic 政策利率 25 個基點至 13.75%，並指出景氣循環產業動能轉弱，且整體與核心通膨均已回落至
目標區間上限以下，為進一步降息提供空間。不過，巴西央行並未承諾下次會議是否將繼續降息，而

是強調將視未來經濟與通膨數據逐次評估。
另一方面，巴西總統大選進入白熱化階段則是當前影響市場最主要的因子。市場民調顯示，相較
7 月時現任總統魯拉在民調中普遍領先競爭者波索納洛約 5 至 8 個百分點 ，在 9 月的民調雙方差距已
縮小至 2 個百分點以內，選情不確定性仍高。若選舉最終由魯拉勝選，其支持財政擴張的政策，將可
能使市場對於巴西財政赤字問題的擔憂加劇，進而使巴西股市面臨較大壓力；而若波索納洛勝選，市
場普遍預期其能提出改善投資人信心的財政政策 ， 為巴西央行創造更多降息空間 ， 有利後續巴西股市
上行。
整體而言，儘管市場對拉美 各國當前升息預期可能過於悲觀，但目前拉美多國經濟成長同步放
緩，加上巴西總統大選選情膠著，選後財政政策方向不確定性高，在考量大選及政策方向不確定性的
狀況下，短線拉美市場波動恐將加劇。

能源類股：全球石油庫存持續下降，但中東石油出口已出現回升
9 月西德州原油期貨價格受美伊衝突升溫影響，一度於月中重新站上每桶 100 美元，其後隨衝突
趨緩，油價漲幅逐步收斂，月底約回落至90 美元附近，能源類股表現亦大致跟隨油價走勢。展望10
月，近期中東石油供應恢復速度不如預期，加上庫存持續下降，預期油價下檔仍具支撐。然而，市場
數據顯示，高油價及供應受阻已同步抑制石油需求，且中東各國原油出口持續回升，後續供應緊張情
況有望逐步改善。因此，雖然中東地緣政治風險仍將對油價形成支撐，但隨美伊重啟外交接觸，油價
進一步大幅上漲的空間預期相對有限。

受到美伊談判僵持影響，國際能源署（IEA）與美國能源資訊署（EIA）在 9 月月報均將中東石油
供應恢復的預期時間延後。IEA 將中東石油供應全面恢復的預期時間延至 2027 年，並將 2026 年全
球石油供應減幅，由上月預估的每日減少 430 萬桶擴大至 570 萬桶；EIA 則將中東石油供給恢復至
衝突前水準的時間，由 2027 年初延至第 2 季。
此外 ，受到中東供應受阻與油價偏高影響 ，IEA 亦將 2026 年全球石油需求預測下修每日 94 萬桶 ，
預估全年需求將較去年減少每日 250 萬桶，EIA 則預估全年石油需求將減少約每日 170 萬桶，石油
輸出國組織（OPEC）也將今年需求由上月預估的每日增加 60 萬桶下修至 40 萬桶。
受此影響，全球石油庫存持續下降。IEA 估計，8 月全球可觀察石油庫存減少 9,500 萬桶，自 2
月底以來已累計下降 5.07 億桶，顯示市場仍持續動用庫存填補缺口。EIA 也預期全球石油庫存將持
續下降至年底，因此將2026 年下半年布蘭特原油均價預測，由上月約每桶82 美元上調至 90 美元；

此外 ，隨著 2027 年中東出口與產量逐步恢復 ，IEA 預估明年全年布蘭特原油均價將回落至 74 美元。
另一方面，中東原油出口則已經出現回升跡象。路透社 9 月 28 日引述 Kpler 初步資料指出，沙
烏地阿拉伯與阿聯酋 9 月原油出口均出現上升 ， 帶動中東主要產油國當月原油日均出口量回升至每日
1,280 萬桶，創下 2 月戰爭爆發以來最高水準。其中，沙烏地阿拉伯9 月原油日均出口預估將達每日
540 萬桶，較 8 月的 244.6 萬桶增加逾一倍。由於沙國東西向輸油管線先前因攻擊受損，沙烏地計
畫將部分原先經紅海延布港出口的原油 ， 轉往波斯灣沿岸港口裝運 ， 帶動拉斯坦努拉港9 月日均出貨
量預估由 8 月的每日 92.9 萬桶增至 360 萬桶。
整體而言，中東供應恢復時程延後，加上全球石油庫存持續下降，短期仍有利支撐油價。然而，
石油需求預估同步下修 ， 且沙烏地阿拉伯與阿聯酋原油出口均已逐步回升 ，後續石油供應緊張程度可
望有所緩解。整體而言，儘管油價仍受中東地緣風險支撐，但隨美國期中選舉接近，近期美伊亦重啟

外交接觸，市場對原油供應中斷的擔憂可望逐步降溫，油價進一步大幅上漲空間相對有限。

黃金類股：升息預期攀升壓抑金價表現；財富配置及央行買盤支撐中長期需求
近期黃金價格表現較為疲弱，主要受到市場升息預期持續攀升，帶動美債殖利率走高，增加持有
黃金的機會成本，對於利率較為敏感的黃金 ETF 需求因此受到壓抑。然而，高盛研究指出，聯準會
近期決議升息雖然延長了市場高利率環境時間，但並不會進一步墊高利率終點 ， 加上全球央行持續購
金，以及投資人從財富配置角度增加黃金配置，均有助於支撐中期黃金需求。
高盛報告指出，聯準會於 9 月升息一碼，增加持有黃金的機會成本，使對利率敏感的黃金ETF 需
求下滑 ， 高盛因此將2026 年底金價預估由每盎司 4,900 美元下修至 4,650 美元 。 然而 ， 高盛也預估，

聯準會將於 2027 年 9 月至 2028 年 3 月期間降息三碼，使政策利率最終回到 3.25%至 3.5%區間，
故儘管聯準會 9 月升息延長了高利率環境，但實際並未墊高利率終點，因此來自黃金 ETF 的賣壓僅
會延後金價上漲時程，但並未改變高盛對黃金的偏多看法。
另一方面，央行買盤仍是金價支撐核心。高盛9 月報告預估，全球央行近三個月經季節調整後的
購金速度已上升至每月 91 噸，遠高於 2022 年前每月 17 噸的平均水準。高盛指出，這股需求主要
來自分散外匯儲備的考量，在 2022 年俄羅斯央行資產遭美國凍結後，各國央行更加重視儲備資產遭
制裁或凍結的風險，相較於對利率敏感的 ETF 資金，央行基於儲備分散而進行的購金，不易因短期
利率變動而轉向，因此即使聯準會升息，央行買盤仍有利支撐金價表現。
除了央行買盤外，花旗也從全球財富配置的角度看好黃金。花旗指出，全球每年新增黃金礦產供
應規模相當有限，若全球家庭額外將 0.1%的財富配置於黃金，就相當於約 4,459 噸黃金購買量，也

等於 1.2 年的新增礦產供應量。換言之，即使全球投資人僅小幅提高黃金配置比重，也可能創造遠超
過新增供給的需求。由於黃金供給短期內難以快速增加，市場往往需要透過金價上漲，吸引既有持有
人釋出黃金以滿足新增需求，因此資產配置需求增加可能對金價產生顯著推升影響。
不過，花旗也指出，黃金新增供給有限的特性雖有助於放大需求對金價的推升效果，但同時也可
能提高金價出現較劇烈修正的風險。花旗估計，2022 年以來的金價上漲已使既有黃金持有人的帳面
財富增加約 13 兆美元，若持有人將其中約 1 兆美元轉回股票、債券或現金，所形成的黃金賣壓便可
能超過目前全部實體黃金需求，故當持有人集中獲利了結時，金價也可能出現較劇烈的修正。
整體而言，在市場升息預期持續攀升的情況下，短期黃金ETF 需求可能受到壓抑，金價仍可能面
臨震盪 。 然而 ， 全球央行基於外匯儲備分散需求持續購金 ， 加上投資人從財富配置角度提高黃金配置 ，
均有助於支撐中期黃金需求。

固定收益：聯準會升息穩定通膨預期，凸顯中天期美債與投資級債吸引力
美國 9 月聯準會貨幣政策會議以 12 比 0 全票通過 ， 將聯邦基金利率目標區間上調 25 個基點至
3.75%–4.00%。最新經濟預測同步將 2026 年核心 PCE 上修至 3.4%、GDP 成長率上修至 2.3%，且
全面上修 2026 至 2027 年政策利率預估至 4.1%，並暗示年底前仍有一次升息空間。9 月聯準會採取
預防性升息，主要是在避免通膨預期鬆動並維持政策公信力，確立高利率維持更久的政策基調。
美國政府於 9 月 30 日公布第二季國內生產毛額(GDP)終值，經季節調整後成長年率由 1.5%上修

至 2.2%，高於經濟學家預估的維持不變。第一季 GDP 季增年率也由 2.1%上修至 2.5%。其中，占
美國經濟活動逾 6 成的消費支出，第二季成長率由 3.4%上修至 3.8%，顯示美國消費動能強勁；元
大投顧指出，美國 8 月核心資本財訂單月增率加速至 1.6%，其中初級金屬、機械及電機設備訂單均
呈現增長，顯示 AI 相關投資及企業設備更新需求持續支撐資本財訂單，企業設備投資可望對經濟成
長形成穩健貢獻。在消費與企業動能表現強勁下，使市場重新評估美國經濟對通膨黏性的影響。
彭博 10 月 1 日的報告指出，在 Market Pulse 調查的 173 名受訪者，超過一半的人預測美國 30
年期公債殖利率在年底將達到 6%。儘管伊朗戰爭和高借貸成本持續影響 ， 美國經濟仍維持強勁成長 ，
這種韌性加劇了通膨和進一步升息的⾵險；人工智慧基礎設施建設競賽引發了企業發債的狂潮 ，推升
長天期債券發行量，全球企業今年以來已發行超過 4000 億美元的債券，用於技術投資，而其中大部
分債券發行於美國。另外，各國政府持續的預算赤字，美國聯邦政府債務規模首次突破 40 兆美元，

美國政府需要擴大發債量 ， 投資人要求更高的利率來購買債券等因素 ，皆為推升美國長天期殖利率上
行的原因。因此，長天期債券後續表現仍須持續觀察利率走勢、債券供需變化及通膨發展。
展望 10 月，由於美國通膨仍高於聯準會 2%的目標，2026 年底有再升息一碼的可能，使得短
天期利率下行空間有限。在政策利率可能維持高檔、長天期殖利率又受到供給面壓力推升的環境下，
當前的債券配置應以「息收防禦」與「存續期間控管」為核心。隨著美國公債利率上行，彭博美國投
資級債指數平均剩餘到期期限持續下降 ， 存續期間縮短有助降低利率變動對債券價格的影響 。美國債
市投資建議重點配置中天期美國公債與投資級公司債 。中天期美國公債與投資級公司債不僅能鎖定具
吸引力的收益率水準，也能兼顧存續期間風險控管，降低利率波動對投資組合淨值的衝擊，在當前環
境下有助於兼顧收益與風險控制。

Industry View In-Line

On Day 3 of the 2026 OCP Global Summit, there is a full-day special focus on
Photonics. Industry participants such as NVIDIA, Meta, Microsoft, Oracle, Broadcom,
Celestica, Arista, Ciena, Amphenol, Lumentum, and Huawei presented their views.
We summarize the key findings below:
1) When the moderator asks key industry players whether they will apply CPO or
NPO, almost all companies answered 'Both'.
2) AI density depends on optical connectivity. With the day's focus on Photonics, the

importance of optical connectivity was well highlighted in each session.
3) Five key metrics for new optical technology: 1) performance, 2) power, 3) cost, 4)
speed to deployment and 5) reliability and serviceability. Huawei believes NPO has
advantages on maintainability, open decoupling, availability and industry
implementation.
4) Scale-in is another new approach to enhance AI density.
5) Root cause of CPO failures: 83-85% of failures due to ELS, 11-17% due to fiber/

connector. Dust and dirt are also common failure issues.
6) Optics vs. Copper: Full Optical Pod is targeted towards scale-up sizes >576
accelerators, Optics and Copper to coexist between 72 and 576 accelerators.

Summit (14 Oct 2026)

Key Takeaways
A full day discussion of Photonics further demonstrated the importance of
optical connectivity.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Earnings upward revision on better OPM, robust
AI ASIC revenue on track; maintain Buy with TP
at NT$6,800
Mediatek will host an analyst meeting on Oct 30; we expect an inline GM/OPM
(guidance mid-point of 46%/15%), yet we expect a strong c30% QoQ growth
outlook for 4Q26 thanks to AI ASIC contribution and better mix/pricing for
consumer products. On the back of solid operating leverage, we are also looking for

OPM upside. We are looking for upbeat 4Q26 guidance.
With AI ASIC starts contributing in 4Q26 and multiplying growth into next two
years, we expect OPM expansion for Mediatek on the back of its operational
leverage. We lift our earnings projection for 2026/2027 /28 by 9%/ 25%/9%. We
maintain our Buy with TP$6,800 (30x of 2027 /28 EPS, from 35x previously, mainly
on recent macro concerns and concerns on AI capex beyond 2028).

### Page 7

Mediatek (2454.TW)
21 October 2026 Citi Research
7
Mediatek
Company description
Mediatek (MTK) is a fabless IC company that designs SoC for smartphone,
smart TV, and digital media devices. It also develops chips for connectivity,
networking, smart home gadgets, and automotive sectors. It was founded in
1997, is listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange, and is headquartered in Taiwan.
It has sales and research subsidiaries in mainland China, Singapore, India,

the US, Japan, Korea, Denmark, and England. It primarily sells its products in
China and other emerging-market countries.
Investment strategy
We rate Mediatek shares as Buy. We expect Mediatek to ride on 5G migration
in emerging markets and market share gain of its high-end Dimensity series in
China market as well. In particular, we see strong growth potential from its AI
ASIC, NTN-satellite business in the long term. We believe solid fundamentals

and strong operating leverage will support Mediatek’s margins outlook.
Valuation
Our NT$6,800 target price (rounded) for Mediatek is based on 30x P/E to
2027 /2028 EPS, which is 1-std above its five-year average forward P/E given
the promising outlook from its AI ASIC business starting from 2H26. We
believe our target PE is also justified by Mediatek's global and regional peers'
trading at an average of c32x PER. AI ASIC development with CSPs should

provide more upside from 2027 onwards. We also see Mediatek as well
prepared for 6G and 6G+NTN Satellite communication. Despite lukewarm
smartphone overall deamand, we expect Mediatek to benefit from 5G
migration in emerging markets and its Dimensity series should also gain
shares in flagship models. Non-smartphone business is also seeing
stabilizing TV and Io T demand with Mediatek outgrowing the overall market
on its better technology in WiFi 7 and connectivity.

Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) weaker-than-expected growth from AI ASIC business; 2)
more severe competition in the smartphone segment; 3) slower overall
demand related to macroeconomic risks, especially for smartphone demand;
and 4) further price hikes from foundries.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.
Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst

expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment
rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient

valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating
and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of

information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price
chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and

investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.

Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in

reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

information. The information contained in this report may differ from information a private company may subsequently make
public or otherwise share with investors. Continuation of publishing private company reports or private company sections
within reports is at Citi's discretion, in accordance with internal policies.

### Page 1

Macquarie Equity Research
26 October 2026
Nan Ya Plastics
Mix upgrade accelerating
Key Points
• NPC reported Sep revenue up 55% YoY while net profit surged over 9x
YoY to TWD35.1bn, with Sep DPS above 2Q26 DPS.
• We expect a rising high-end product contribution to accelerate Low Dk/
Low CTE glass fibre sales next year.
• Our industry checks suggest that CCL supply will remain tight due to
glass fibre shortages.
• Upside to our 4Q26 forecast. NPC reported strong Sep earnings
following a required disclosure on unusual share-price movements.
Revenue rose 55% YoY to TWD34.4bn, while net profit surged over
9x to TWD35.1bn. Sep EPS reached TWD4.42, above 2Q26 quarterly
EPS of TWD3.37. Mgmt attributed the strength mainly to electronic
materials, including high-end CCL benefiting from AI demand. We
estimate Sept EBIT at TWD8–9bn, ~45% of 3Q26 EBIT, implying upside to
our TWD20.3bn 4Q EBIT estimate.
• Rising high-end CCL contribution in 2027/28. The company refuted
market rumours that its M9 material had failed customer validation,
noting that advanced CCL expansion remains on track, with new capacity
fully dedicated to high-end products. We expect stronger growth in Low
Dk/Low CTE glass fibres, with high-end products reaching 40% of the mix
vs 10% this year as CSP orders ramp up next year. We also expect M9
CCL to pass validation in 1H27.
• Q-Cloth and PTFE not ready yet. Our checks at Taiwan TPCA Show
2026 with glass fibre and CCL players suggest demand is multiple times
industry capacity, with glass-fibre supply the key bottleneck given long
capacity and weaving-machine ramp-up times. As producers shift
capacity towards Low Dk/T-Cloth, E-Cloth supply should remain tight. Q-
Cloth remains constrained by high costs and poor flexibility, while Low
Dk glass fibre with HC Resin can deliver similar performance. PTFE is
also unlikely to threaten glass fibre near term given its low mechanical
strength.
Earnings changes: No changes.
Valuation: We maintain our NT$506.00 TP based on an unchanged 20x
FY27E PER.
Catalysts: 2H26 high-end CCL ramp-up and capacity expansion, glass fibre
capacity additions, and stronger equity-method earnings from 2027.
Investment Thesis and Recommendation
We expect high-end CCL qualifications, mix upgrades and utilisation gains
to drive earnings growth, supported by recovering legacy businesses and
higher associate earnings.

Macquarie Equity Research Nan Ya Plastics
Key Risks to Investment Thesis
• Slower-than-expected AI data centre capex.
• Delays in, or failure of, high-end CCL qualification.
• Weaker-than-expected ASP and utilisation ramp.
• Normalisation of Middle East EG supply
• Earnings volatility at Nan Ya Technology and Formosa
Petrochemical.
Company Description
Nan Ya Plastics is a core member of the Formosa Plastics
Group. The company operates four business lines, including

plastics, chemicals, polyester, and electronic materials,
across Taiwan, China, and the US. Nan Ya also holds equity-
method stakes in Nan Ya Technology (2408 TT) and Formosa
Petrochemical (6505 TT), and consolidates PCB exposure
through Nan Ya PCB (8046 TT).

Key Quant Findings
The quant model currently holds a reasonably positive
view on Nan Ya Plastics. The strongest style exposure is
Price Momentum, indicating this stock has had strong
medium to long term returns which often persist into
the future. The weakest style exposure is Profitability,
indicating this stock is not efficiently converting
investments to earnings; proxied by ratios like ROE or ROA.

### Page 2

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - Unimicron Technology 
Unimicron is a key supplier of ABF substrates (25% market share in 2025) and BT 
substrates and also produces FPC/HDI/PCB products. We note (1) over 70% of ABF 
shipments are covered by long-term agreements (LTA), which limits pricing flexibility and 
makes price hikes less likely; (2) the company’s execution track record has been weaker 
than peers; (3) capacity expansion plan remains slower than peers, constraining its 
ability to capture incremental AI demand. The current ABF substrates market is seeing 
more favorable supply demand outlook, and Unimicron is likely to see a better pricing 
outlook to reflect rising material costs in the coming quarters. AI ABF accounts for 10% 
of the company’s total revenue, but the growth is likely to be slower than peers as the 
company is losing market share given its less favourable production yield and slow 
capacity expansion. Overall, we are Neutral rated on Unimicron given our outlook for 
limited upside potential, barring ASIC AI servers, despite our generally positive view in 
the long term on its profitability trajectory. 
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Unimicron Technology 
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$1,270 for Unimicron is 
based on 17.4x 2028E P/E, which is 2.7x STDV higher than the past upcycle average P/E 
(10.3x).  
Key upside/downside risks: (1) Slower/faster-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2) 
Slower/faster-than-expected ABF substrates upgrading pace, and (3) Slower/faster 
than-expected AI server PCB market share loss progress.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

In Taiwan, we prefer TUC over EMC for greater pricing upside in
the next 12 months, given its higher exposure to midrange/low-
end CCL. Both stocks are attractively valued: EMC at 11x CY28e
P/E and TUC at 9x.
EMC (2383.TW)
• EMC has introduced a new expansion plan for its total CCL capacity to reach
11.95mn sheets per month (SPM) in 2029 and 15.25mn in 2030 (+14% and

+28% y/y). See Exhibit 3  for EMC's 2026-2030 capacity plans.
• EMC's substrate CCL capacity currently sits at 300K SPM. The company
expects to add another 900K SPM in 2H27, reaching 1.2M SPM by end-2027.
Our supply chain checks suggest that a US hyperscaler  will be the first key
customer of EMC's ABF substrate CCLs for its  eighth-generation ASIC (see
our  TPCA takeaways).
• Management noted a growing trend of customers requesting long-term
agreements that span at least three years, on average. These L TAs now

secure volume rather than fixing prices; they do not require prepayments
from customers. Despite the flexibility in pricing and payment, customers
will still face punishment if they fail to pull their promised volumes.
• We estimate that 4Q revenue will grow ~19% q/q, to NT$72.4bn (+190% y/y),
supported by pricing adjustments and continued mix improvement, given
limited capacity increase in 4Q. We see GM expanding 220bps q/q, to 39.9%,
for EPS of NT$53.33 (+32% q/q).

TUC (6274.TWO)
• Management expects price hikes to continue into 4Q, with new pricing for its
M7/8 CCL taking effect from November onwards. For M6 and below, pricing
will remain driven by raw material cost pass-through amid supply tightness.
We expect this trend to persist into 2027, with HVLP4 copper foil, E glass,
and LDK2 glass cloth to be the most undersupplied.
• We believe recent market concerns about TUC's "share loss" for an ASIC
customer are overdone. Given the ongoing supply tightness in the high-end

CCL market, it is normal for CSPs to adopt multi-sourcing strategies to
diversify supply risks. Meanwhile, we believe TUC is actively engaging and
sampling high-end M8+ grade materials with other major CSP customer(s)
for next-gen AI projects. TUC's share in a key satellite customer's next-gen
satellite products will potentially expand in 2027 as well, with materials
upgraded to M7/8.
• We expect 4Q revenue to grow 15% q/q, to NT$20.6bn (+126% y/y), driven

by pricing adjustments and capacity addition at TUC's site in Thailand site
(+100K SPM in 4Q, +200K SPM in 3Q). We see GM expanding 50bps q/q, to
37.4%, for EPS of NT$15.82 (+20% q/q).

companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Price Target Discussion and Valuation Methodology
Our price target for EMC increases ~5%, to NT$8,800, driven by increases in our

2026-28 earnings estimates: Our new price target implies 20x CY28e P/E (unchanged).
Our price target is also our base case scenario value. It is derived from our residual income
(RI) model (similar to the rest of our tech hardware coverage).
To calculate the value of the stock, we use the company's beginning equity plus the
present value of all expected residual income of earnings in excess of cost of capital. The
RI is positive when ROAE is above the cost of capital and negative when it is below the

cost of capital. Our key assumptions (all unchanged) include a cost of equity of 9.3% (beta
of 1.0, equity risk premium of 8.7%, and risk-free rate of 1.0%), a medium-term growth rate
of 12%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
The 20x CY28 P/E implied by our price target is slightly above the past three years'
average +1SD of 18x, but much lower than the 32x recent P/E peak. We deem this
valuation fair because EMC is benefiting from an earnings growth cycle fueled by stronger

AI server CCL demand, EMC's own mix improvement, and continued pricing adjustments,
which should support margin expansion.
Our bull and bear case values also increase ~5%, to NT$13,650 and NT$4,200,
respectively.

TUC: Earnings Estimate Changes
We raise our  gross margin and operating margin assumptions for CY26-28 to factor in the
margin beat in 3Q26. However, we also revise our tax rate assumptions from 4Q26
onwards to reflect the higher-than-expected tax rate in 3Q26 (31.1% vs. our 25.0%
estimate). Our  EPS estimates consequently change +3% for CY26, -5% for CY27, and -6%
for CY28.

Price target discussion and valuation methodology
Our price target for TUC decreases  ~6%, to NT$2,550, primarily driven by the changes
in our earnings estimates for CY27-28: This implies 28x CY27e or 17x CY28e P/E. Our
price target is also our base case scenario value. It is derived from our residual income (RI)
model (similar to the rest of our tech hardware coverage).
To calculate the value of the stock, we use the company's beginning equity plus the

present value of all expected residual income of earnings in excess of cost of capital. The
RI is positive when ROAE is above the cost of capital and negative when it is below the
cost of capital. Our key assumptions (all unchanged) include a cost of equity of 9.3% (beta
of 1.0, equity risk premium of 8.7%, and risk-free rate of 1.0%), a medium-term growth rate
of 11%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
The 17x CY28e P/E implied by our price target is roughly in line with the past three years'

average +1SD of 17.8x. We deem this valuation fair because TUC is benefiting from an
earnings growth cycle fueled by stronger AI server/networking CCL demand, TUC's own
mix improvement, and continued pricing adjustments, which should support margin
expansion.
Our bull and bear case scenario values also decline ~6%, to NT$3,825 and NT$635,
respectively.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

研 究  報 告                
晶呈科技(4768.TT) 
產業：化學工業 
氣體原料量產降本效應與玻璃加工產品放量，推動明年獲利高成⻑ 
 投資評價與建議 
考量三廠C4F6⼀級合成放量顯著優化⽑利率重回30%，且AI驅動TGV及CPO
加工產品步入規模化量產，⻑期動能強勁，參考台股半導體特化與先進封裝概
念股本益比30-35倍，因短期受高額研發費用投入、新廠折舊與轉虧為盈初期
致使當前本益比失真，故採用TGV產線滿載Forward PER。 
 重點評論分析 
特氣出貨回升，TGV量產放量：推估4Q26隨特氣出貨回升及客⼾去庫存告⼀
段落，營收可望季增73.7%至6.6億元，全年營收估增50.9%至18.6億元，玻璃
加工受惠AI高階封裝與CPO需求，GCB及One Piece Glass FAU已進入規模量
產，預估2H26營收較上半年成⻑230%，惟三廠延至4Q26試量產，特氣原料
成本改善遞延，⽑利率下修至23%，加上研發費用增加，使稅後EPS 0.29元。  
⼀級合成與高⽑利產品帶動獲利躍升： 預估2027年營收年增35.1%至25.1億
元，受惠AI ASIC大廠特氣需求放量、TGV及矽光子共同封裝相關應用擴產6倍
產能，並拓展生醫檢測市場，預計3Q27末相關產線陸續量產，Stripper隨認證
客⼾增加至9家，⽑利率因三廠C4F6⼀級合成與純化工廠預計1Q27放量，年
產能250噸，可降低外購原料成本，加上Stripper產能利用率提升，以及TGV、
生醫檢測及Stripper等高⽑利產品營收占比提升至20%，帶動年增7.92ppts至
30.9% ， 營益率升至12.5% ， 稅前盈餘年增936%至2.91億元 ， 稅後EPS 4.30元 。  
 投資風險 
⼀級合成新廠量產進度與高⽑利產品TGV 、 特化投產或客⼾導入進度不如預期 。

### Page 2

2 
| 公司簡介  
公司所做的玻璃通孔技術(Through Glass Via ,TGV)主要應用於大積體電路玻璃載板(Glass Core Su bstrate, 
GCS)(非 Glass interposer)，是做為⼀個玻璃核心晶圓加工廠(TGV Glass Foundry)，並將加工好 的玻璃核心層
(Glass Core Board, GCB)，交付給下游 IC 載板廠或封測廠後，在玻璃核心的外側壓合 ABF 絕緣膜，並製作精細
的銅線路 ， 組合成完整的 GCS ． 亦透過玻璃加工技術開發⼀體成型式 CPO 光纖束導基座(One Piece Glass FAU)。 
GCB 是數個晶片叢共用的超大玻璃地基，支撐所有晶片和並保持封裝的平坦和穩定，解決穩定性(平坦度與結構支
撐)的瓶頸，位置在整個封裝體的最底層，玻璃材料解決熱膨脹、曲蹺度、以及對封裝尺寸的要求極限，目前公司
的製作玻璃微孔的貫穿深度與孔道寬度(孔徑)的比值已達到深寬比 100:1，全孔立體型態增圓度可達 99%、板厚
可達 1.85mm，單位面積產值相較 Glass Interposer 多 25-30 倍，在硬脆且易碎的玻璃材質上實現百倍深寬比，
是公司透過獨家氣態分解融蝕技術，在微觀垂直孔道上的極致控制力。 
One Piece Glass FAU 是⼀種應用於 CPO(共同封裝光學)與超高速光通訊系統中光電整合模組，超微細光纖束的
精準對位組裝，專為插裝與固定直徑僅 125 微米(µm)的超微細光纖束所設計(直徑極細，大約三根光纖才等於⼀
根頭髮的粗細)，在 AI 晶片架構從傳統電訊號傳輸轉向光訊號傳輸的過程中，該產品主要解決(1)微米級光纖對位
極難與光訊號損耗問題，公司雷射改質與氣態蝕刻技術(LADY)，在玻璃上打造出高⼀致性的微孔結構，提供高精
度的光纖對位，顯著提高光耦合效率(Optical Coupling Efficiency)，可減少多件式組裝產生的累積公差(2)高溫運
轉下的熱膨脹與光軸偏斜，利用玻璃材料極低熱膨脹係數的物理特性，確保光纖對位結構在⻑期高溫運算下仍保
持極佳的尺寸，(3)光軸穩定性以及光電整合元件的結構精密性，採用⼀體成型(One-Piece)的玻璃基座設計，結合
TGV 製程的高垂直度與高精度特性，配合客⼾在 AI 系統架構升級中對光電整合元件的規模化部署需求。 
圖表、Glass Core Board (左圖)不同象限與One Piece Glass FAU(右圖)顯微鏡下示意圖

資料來源：玉山投顧、公司資料 
| 營  運  概  況  
外銷與特氣出貨拉動累計營業收入倍增，受費用提升致⽑利率承壓：1H26 合併營收 8.21 億元，YoY+141%，主
要受惠特殊氣體外銷出口的大幅增加，氣體出貨支數達 13,022 支，其中 2Q26 營收 QoQ-13.9%至 3.8 億元，主
因在於部分外銷出口出貨量有所減少至 6,294 支，受韓國等記憶體客⼾要求調整出貨影響，但⽑利率
QoQ+11.1ppts 至 24.4%，因低⽑利產品(海外記憶體客⼾的特氣)相對 1Q26 出貨較少，1H26 ⽑利率 YoY-
20.45ppts 至 18.5%，除了產品組合關係，另因上游⼀級原料合成⾃給率尚未開出，使得原料成本負擔較沉重，
2Q26 費用率從 17.6%提升至 25.6%，主要來⾃研究發展費用大幅提升從 7.1%提升到 12.2%，目的在於為竹北五
廠、頭份三廠等新廠建置進行關鍵技術研發，1H26 稅前虧損 0.36 億元，稅後 EPS -0.99 元。 
特氣與 TGV 放量帶動 2H26 營收優於 1H26：3Q26 單季營收 3.8 億元，ＱoQ-0.6%，推估 3Q26 鋼瓶出貨量
6,000-6,200 支，主要來⾃ C4F6 內外銷出貨持續，⽑利率維持 24%，推估 4Q26 營收 QoQ+73.7%至 6.6 億元， 
4Q26 鋼瓶出貨量 7,500-7,800 支，2H26 鋼瓶出貨合計約 13,500-14,000 支，比 1H26 出貨量成⻑約 7.7%，隨
著全球半導體大廠的新產能與新廠陸續開出，帶動對氣體 C4F6 蝕刻氣體、Laser Gas 等的剛性需求持續增加，另

### Page 3

3 
外 2H26 隨客⼾去庫存化使需求回補，帶動氣體鋼瓶出貨量提升，並受惠於 AI 伺服器與高階封裝和矽光子共同封
裝(Co-Packaged Optics, CPO)，使玻璃加工市場快速成⻑，帶動相關特殊氣體的需求同步提升。 
玻璃加工技術在 TGV Glass Foundry 領域應用，已成功建置兩大可量產的核心產品線，配合年底 2 條產線(月產
能約 1 萬片)到位與量產放量，預估 2H26 營收相較 1H26 成⻑ 230%，主因為 GCB 與 One Piece Glass FAU 皆
已跨越工程驗證，正式進入單月接單與出貨片數達四位數以上的規模量產的放量階段，預估單片 150x150mm(5
萬孔/片)價值約$500-700 美元。 
特化產品 Stripper 目前設有⼀條年產能 288 頓產線，目前已被客⼾認證達 15 個品項，其中 7 個已達穩定交貨，
驗證通過的客⼾數已增加至９家，預估 2H26 營收相較 1H26 成⻑ 3%，加計其他產品，預估 2026 全年營收成⻑
率下修至 50.9%達 18.5 億元 ， 由於三廠(C4F6 ⼀級合成工廠)投產時程 ， 從先前 3Q26 延後至 4Q26 進行試量產 ，
使全年⽑利率從原先預估的 35%下修至 23%，加上研發費用擴張，下修稅前盈餘年成⻑ 41.3%至 0.28 億元，稅
後E P S  0 . 2 9元 。  
⼀級合成放量大幅優化特氣成本結構，TGV 導入 AI 大廠放量，帶動獲利大幅成⻑：邁入 2027 年，預估全年營收
成⻑ 35.1%至 25.1 億元，主要成⻑來⾃特殊氣體核心業務持續受惠 AI ASIC 大廠需求放量，另外玻璃加工應用於
AI 先進封裝與矽光子共同封裝產品預計於 3Q27 末累計建置達 12 條產線並正式量產，推動營收規模呈指數級成
⻑，在生醫檢測應用(竹北五廠)的最新進程上，已於生醫園區簽約購入竹北五廠房，將玻璃加工技術應用於生產生
醫用檢測儀器，在玻璃培養⽫微雕出高⼀致性的 3D 微孔陣列，模擬人體內真實的立體腫瘤微環境(3D 全立體型
態載具(Booster))，針對 EGFR 基因突變陽性的局部晚期非小細胞肺癌細胞進行精準控制併用標靶藥物泰格沙
(Tagrisso®)之細胞檢測實驗體外評估(In Vitro) ， 提升藥物對癌細胞的抑制與殺傷成效 ， 並藉由開發的生醫檢測儀
器，作為癌症精準用藥的關鍵數據，預計五廠將於 3Q27 末正式開始量產，此應用將使玻璃加工技術跨足高⽑利
生醫市場，現有廠房空間至少可規劃 6 條產線，後續將視客⼾需求進行滾動擴充。Stripper 隨 2026 年認證客⼾
增加朝向產能滿載，以及二廠無水氟化氫(AHF)量產線與三氯化硼(BCl3)試產線裝機完成，四條產線年產值上看 3-
4 億元的效益下，穩定貢獻營收。2027 全年⽑利率則受惠頭份三廠的 C4F6 ⼀級合成與純化工廠預計於 1Q27 正
式放量，設計年產能 250 噸，特氣⾃給率將大幅拉升，改變過去依賴外購二、三級純化原料的成本結構，使成本
大幅降低，加上特用化學品業務在產能利用率拉升，高⽑利產品如 TGV 玻璃載板、生醫檢測儀器、Stripper ⾃製
放量的佔比合計提升至 20%，使⽑利率顯著提升 7.92ppts 至 30.9%，費用率隨營業收入規模擴大而稀釋至 18.36%，
營益率提升 9.75ppt 至 12.5%，稅前盈餘年成⻑ 936%至 2.91 億元，稅後 EPS 4.30 元。

6426 統新 
(Apogee Optocom Co.)

報告日期： 2026/10/19 研 究 員：方姿婷  (Lesley Fang) 
壹、投 資 建 議 
重點摘要： 
 統新 2Q26 營收為 2.08 億元，+34.7%QoQ、+82.4%YoY，毛利率
躍升至 56.20%，稅後淨利 0.86 億元，單季稅後 EPS+2.22 元。
2Q26 受惠生產效率提升、產能滿載毛利率躍升。 
 AI 伺服器帶動統新的 DWDM 產品延伸至 AI 資料中心 DCI 主要
骨幹濾片，光纖波段分離與光纖傳輸頻寬增加，會需要統新薄
膜濾光片作為訊號傳輸強化。除 Scale-Across 長距離骨幹，分
散式 AI 數據中心，專門做長距離光通訊傳輸 DWDM 市場，
2026~2031 年每年維持 31%年複合成長。 
 1.6T 光纖收發模組 ， 未來 2 年呈倍數增長 。 1.6T 光纖收發模組，
在後端 PD(接收端)改採 200G PD，會用到 8 片統新之薄膜濾光
片。

一. 統新 2Q26 營收與財報優於市場預期，3Q26 續強 
統新 2Q26 營收為 2.08 億元，+34.7%QoQ、+82.4%YoY，毛利率躍
升至 56.20%，稅後淨利 0.86 億元，單季稅後 EPS+2.22 元。主要受
惠於 AI 資料中心互連需求，使得統新產線維持滿載。展望 3Q26
預估統新營收為 2.42 億元，+16.35%QoQ，毛利率 56.58%，稅後淨
利 1.06 億元，單季稅後 EPS+2.76 元。統新預計產能增加 30%，以
因應客戶需求。

二. 2026 年 DWDM 隨 AI 骨幹網絡建置，濾片量增價漲 
統新高密度波長多工器( D W D M，D e n s e  W a v e l e n g t h  D i v i s i o n  
Multiplexing)，至少可傳輸 16 種不同顏色的光纖，最高可傳輸 128
種不同顏色之光纖，同一條主幹網路上，可以傳輸的頻寬因此加
大，相當具有傳輸效率。DWDM 於發送端將不同波長的光信號組
合起來(覆用, MUX)，並耦合到光纜線路上的同一根光纖中，進行
傳輸，在接收端又將多個波長的光信號分開(解覆用, DEMUX)，並
作進一步處理，恢復出原波長信號後送入不同的終端接收器。統
新的 DWDM 產品延伸至 AI 資料中心 DCI 主要骨幹濾片，波段分
離 ( S -B a n d、C - B a n d 傳 輸 核 心、L -B a nd 容 量 擴 充、S -B a nd 未 來 預 留、
U-Band 8nm 用於 OTDR 光纖監控偵測主幹網路是否具有斷點或需
要救援)，統新以單通道 100GHz 去細分 DWDM 濾光片，統新推出
新品，50GHz 間隔濾光片，支援的通道數量提升至 96Channels。 
投資評等：買進 
目標價位：375.00 元

三.  AI 伺服器高速傳輸，帶動光通訊主被動元件量增 
Scale-Across 長距離骨幹，分散式 AI 數據中心，專門做長距離光
通訊傳輸 DWDM 市場，2026~2031 年，DWDM 市場每年維持 31%
年 複合 成長。此 外，受惠 AI 伺服 器傳 輸速 率提升，如：Broadcom、
Marvell 等國際晶片廠商，於 2026 年起已推出 800G /1.6T Switch 晶
片組，等同於 AI 伺服器後端 Ports 支援的光纖通訊傳輸會一併升
級至 800G 與 1.6T 傳輸速率。Yole 報告指出，1.6T 光纖收發模組
出貨量，2026 年/2027 年/2028 年預估量 15~20M/55~60M/85~100M，
1.6T 光纖收發模組 ， 未來 2 年內呈倍數增長 。 1.6T 光纖收發模組，
在後端 PD(接收端)改採 200G PD ， 會用到 8 片統新之薄膜濾光片。
在薄膜濾光片鍍膜層數 ， Scale-Across 與 NPO 所需薄膜濾光片層數
約 150L~200L，未來發展至 CPO 時鍍膜層數會再增加 8L，統新為
此預計多準備 10 條產線預計開發 250L+規格，予台系知名晶圓代
工廠商。統新指出，Scale-Out 濾片鍍膜代工量增加，是替中系系
統廠代工(層數約 100~150L)。Scale-Across 濾片鍍膜層數會大於
200L，甚至高達 250L，目前訂單能見度高，涵蓋 1.)同業產能難以
支應，Cohr 交給統新、2.)中系系統廠商加單、3.)台系網通廠商於
低軌衛星鍍膜(LEO 非光學鍍膜) 。統新毛利率之所以跳升，是先前
客戶給統新的試產量僅 20~30 片，2Q26 起量產量增至 K 級別，出
貨量增加、規模經濟效應之下，單月營收損平點(評估為 0.4~0.5
億元)，2Q26 起月營收顯著跳升至 0.6~0.7 億元。預期此營運動能
至少能見度到 4Q26，但由於 DWDM 交期延長至 6~8 個月，預估
2027 年產業供需偏向緊俏。

四.投資建議：買進，目標價 375 元 
統新競爭優勢在於其生產高品質高效率之薄膜濾光片篩選特定輻
射波段的光學元件，能篩選光的強度、改變光譜成分或限定震動
截面，藉由 CWDM/DWDM 技術使相同數據集中於同一根光纖中 
傳輸，增大傳輸容量進而提高光纖的使用效率，生產良率逐步提
升且具佔有一定之市場地位。預估統新 2026 年營收 8.56 億元，
+75.77%YoY，毛利率 53.43%，稅後淨利 3.32 億元，稅後 EPS 為
+8.63 元。展望 2027 年，隨產能積極擴充之下，預估統新年營收
為 17.02 億元，+98.83%YoY ，毛利率預估為 54.85% ，稅後淨利 5.57
億元，稅後 EPS+14.47 元。考量基礎建設帶動 DWDM 規格升級，
AI 伺服器傳輸速率提升因應 1.6T 傳輸薄膜濾光片用量同步增長，
給予統新投資建議買進，目標價 375 元(26.00 X2027 EPS)。

一.公司簡介

統新(6426)為生產精密光學元件，以及高密度分波多工器(DWDM)
關鍵元件─薄膜濾光片(TFF)之專業廠商。各產品線營收占比為主
動光學元件佔比低於 15%、被動光學元件：80~85%、光學鍍膜元
件與代工低於 10%。主要產品 DWDM 濾光片 (Dense Wavelength 
Division Multiplexer，即為高密度波長多工分工器)技術可以大幅提
高光纖通訊系統之頻寬，成為光纖通訊系統的市場中關注的焦
點。統新國內主要競爭對手為東典光電科技(股)，國外廠商包含
II-IV Cohernet(美國)、Iridian Technologies(加拿大)等廠商投入相關
產品研發及生產。

圖一、光通訊元件

資料來源：公司、宏遠投顧整理

二.26 年營運概況及 26 年展望 
1.統新 DWDM 技術層層堆疊良率優異  
統新主要產品為薄膜濾光片即為篩選特定輻射波段的光學元件，
能篩選光的強度、改變光譜成分或限定震動截面，藉由不同光線
原理而選擇通過的光源(紅、藍、綠光) ，且藉由 CWDM/DWDM 技
術使相同數據集中於同一根光纖中傳輸，增大傳輸容量進而提高
光纖的使用效率。

2.因應 AI 資料中心基建商機，薄膜濾光片產品走向更高規  
回顧 5G 時期的薄膜濾光片，支援頻段增加，切割更細，中心波

波長 6 組延伸至 12 組 ， 每一道光波段間距縮短至 3.5nm 甚至更
短，薄膜濾波濾光片用量增加，設計難度提升。隨著支援頻段愈
切分愈細，波段之間間距愈來愈短，需要用的薄膜濾波片數量倍

可望取得一定市場地位。

## Document 257: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-09-17
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

00

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다 
기다렸던 4 행정 중속 엔진 캐파 증설 단행 
HD현대중공업의 4행정 엔진 캐파는 기존 3.0GW에서 증설이 완료되는 '28년부터 
램프업을 거쳐 '30년 7.2GW까지 확대될 계획이다. ①[육상 발전용]은 신공장(울산 
온산) 3GW 와 HD 현대엔진(전남 영암) 1GW 를 합쳐 4.0GW, ②[선박용]은 
2.3GW에서 3.2GW로 확대된다. HD현대엔진 캐파는 기존 0.7GW에서 1GW까
지 확대되며, 해당 라인은 신공장이 가동되기 전 ‘27~’28 년 구간에서의 선박&육상 엔
진 모두 대응 가능하다. 신공장 캐파는 총 3.0GW로, ‘28년 약 30%의 가동률로 시
작해 램프업을 거쳐 ‘30 년부터 풀캐파로 20MW급 모델을 생산할 예정이다. 4 행정 
중속엔진에 대한 수요 가시성은 높다. 동사가 공유했던 육상 발전 엔진 인콰이어리 규
모는 기존 약 3~9GW였으나, 현재는 이를 크게 상회하는 수준으로 소통하고 있다.

최근 부유식 데이터센터(FDC)에 대한 하이퍼스케일러들의 인콰이어리 또한 확대되고 
있다. HD 한국조선해양은 최근 미국 선급(ABS)로부터 100MW급 규모의 부유식 발
전 설비(BMPP)에 대한 기본 인증(AiP)를 획득했으며, 동사는 FDC 신조 사업과 더불
어 발전원 4 행정 엔진을 파워십 및 부유식 발전설비(BMPP)에 납품하며 그룹사들과 
패키지 사업으로 진행할 예정이다. 가시화된 수요를 기반으로 엔진기계사업부의 구조적 
성장 보여줄 것으로 전망하며, 이에 따른 리레이팅을 예상한다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 및 목표주가 기존 97 만원 유지 
HD 현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’ 및 목표주가 기존 97 만원을 유지한다. 동사는 
올해 상선수주 목표 이미 초과달성하며 안정적인 펀더멘털 유지 중이다. 하반기 남은 
기간은 LNGC, VLGC/VLAC 중심의 선별 수주 전략을 통해 ‘28 년 이후의 상선 부
문 수익성 유지 충분히 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 글로벌 지정학적 리스크 확대에 따른 
해군 현대화 사이클 기조에 맞추어 미 해군은 동맹국의 호위함 구매를 고려하고 있으
며, MASGA를 기반으로 중장기적으로 미국 전투함을 건조하기 위해 HD현대의 미국 
진출 가능성 높다는 판단이다. 또한 미국을 제외한 글로벌 함정(페루, 필리핀, 말레이시
아, 칠레, 사우디, 이집트 등) 수주 파이프라인도 다수 보유하고 있어 함정 모멘텀에 따
른 리레이팅 충분히 가능할 전망이다. 특수선+4 행정 엔진+FDC+SMR 등 조선소에 
붙일 수 있는 모든 추가 성장동력을 보유한 유일한 종목이기에 조선업 최선호주(Top-
Pick)로 제시한다. 최근 불거지고 있는 노조 파업 문제 해소될 시 저점 매수 기회라는 
판단이다.

## Document 289: Australian Business Conditions and Confidence, September (2026-10-29)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Australian Business Conditions and Confidence, September (2026-10-29).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#businessconditions #confidence #aud #australia
Australian Business Conditions and Confidence, September
By Luka Belobrajdic
Economist, Westpac
14:15 October 13 2026 Share
Confidence posts its sharpest fall since the start of the Middle East conflict as elevated fuel
prices and higher interest rates weigh on business sentiment, while conditions hold steady
at weak levels. Conditions unchanged at -1 / Confidence -4pts to -13.

### Page 2

The September NAB Business Survey was in the field from 23 to 30 September, capturing ongoing
global uncertainty, still-rising fuel costs and, at least in part, the RBA’s September rate hike. Business
confidence continued to deteriorate, recording its sharpest fall since March and the onset of the Middle
East conflict. The index declined 4pts to -13 and, while remaining above the lows recorded earlier this
year, is now at its weakest level since May. At 18pts below its long-run average, the result underscores

the deep pessimism that continues to weigh on businesses. This broadly mirrors the pessimism evident
in the Westpac-Melbourne Institute Consumer Sentiment Index, where elevated fuel prices and higher
interest rates have coalesced in a similarly weak assessment of the outlook.
The headline business conditions index held steady in negative territory at -1, albeit rising 0.25pts on
an unrounded basis. While encouraging that conditions have not deteriorated further, the index has

now remained at -1 (rounded) for two consecutive months, its weakest level since the height of the
COVID-19 pandemic in 2020. At 7pts below its long-run average, the result points to continued
pressure across the business sector.
Across the major sub-components, employment moved into negative territory, falling 3pts to -1 and
sitting 4pts below its long-run average. This marks the first outright negative reading since November
2020 and provides further evidence of the gradual easing in labour market conditions. Against the

backdrop of compressed margins, the result may also indicate firms are becoming increasingly
cautious around hiring decisions. By contrast, trading conditions rose 1pt to 4, although they remain
8pts below their long-run average. The relative resilience in trading conditions is broadly consistent with

### Page 3

Westpac DataX Card-Tracker data, which suggests nominal spending remained resilient, albeit
slowing, through the September quarter despite ongoing headwinds facing consumers. Profitability
recorded the strongest improvement across the major components, rising 3pts to -5, although the sub-
index remains deeply subdued at 10pts below its long-run average.
Capacity utilisation edged 0.2ppt lower to 82.3%. While the monthly decline provides a tentative signal

of easing capacity pressures, utilisation remains 0.9ppt above its long-run average and continues to
trend higher on a three-month average basis. Capital expenditure continued to recover from its mid-
year weakness, rising 3pts to 7. While still below the highs recorded in the first quarter, the sub-index is
now trending higher on a three-month average basis, pointing to some stabilisation in investment
intentions.
Cost growth was comparatively subdued in September, although purchase cost pressures remain

elevated. Purchase cost growth edged 0.1ppt higher to 2.4%qtr, sitting 1.1ppt above its long-run
average and continuing to weigh on margins. By contrast, labour cost growth eased 0.1ppt to 1.7%qtr,
although it remains 0.5ppt above its long-run average. Final product price growth was unchanged at
0.8%qtr, 0.2ppt above its long-run average. The widening gap between purchase cost and final product
price growth suggests firms continue to absorb a meaningful share of higher input costs, consistent

with profitability remaining deeply below average. Looking ahead, the October survey will provide an
early indication of how the card surcharging ban is affecting business costs and the extent to which
these are passed through to final prices.

### Page 4

Related articles
Among other indicators, forward orders declined 1pt to -1, pointing to continued caution around the
demand outlook. Inventories, by contrast, rose 4pts (rounded) to 7, some 5pts above their long-run
average. In combination with weak forward orders, the inventory build points to a degree of slack
emerging across the business sector.

## Document 302: Capital Market Outlook (2026-10-05)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Capital Market Outlook (2026-10-05).pdf
Report source: Merrill Lynch (CMO)
Date: 2026-10-05
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Stronger Growth, Costlier Money
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
The latest GDP report and annual revisions show a sturdier U.S. economy than previously
reported. According to the Bureau of Economic Analysis (BEA), real GDP grew at a 2.2%
annualized pace in Q2 compared to 1.5% previously estimated, and Q1 was revised
from 2.1% to 2.5%. Both 2024 and 2025 were also revised slightly higher. Most of the

first half of the year revision was in categories related to the Artificial Intelligence (AI)
buildout, but real consumer spending has also been revised slightly higher, accelerating
from 0.7% annualized growth in Q1 to 3.8% in Q2 despite surging energy prices and
slow hiring.
Spending in July and August remained much stronger than labor market conditions alone
would suggest, also averaging 3.7% annualized growth. Upside revisions to net interest

income and personal saving suggest households have benefited from more income than
was previously apparent, with personal saving revised higher back to 2023. The new data,
according to the BEA, show the January to July 2026 savings rate averaged 4.8% versus
3.3% prerevision, strengthening consumer spending underpinnings despite moderate
hiring, high energy prices and rising borrowing costs.
The BEA update also included inflation methodological changes related to categories

inflated by the AI boom—such as portfolio management fees and computer software and
accessories. These improved the inflation picture to some extent—August “core” PCE
inflation was reduced by 0.3 ppts to 3% YoY, bringing it closer to “core” consumer price
index inflation, which was 2.5% YoY and 2% on a three-month annualized basis. While still
above the 2% Federal Reserve (Fed) target, “core” PCE now appears stable at 3% year over
year since January rather than accelerating. These methodological changes are expected

to dampen pass-through of AI-related price pressures into “core” inflation and effects of
stock-market swings on inflation.
They also produced an encouraging improvement in recent PCE inflation momentum.
Three-month annualized “core” PCE inflation slowed from 4.3% at the peak of tariff
effects in February to 2.1% in August. For durable goods, this rate was down from 5.8%
in February to 1.5% in August, and, for services ex-energy and healthcare, it slowed from

4.2% to 2.2%, for example. Inflation is volatile on a three-month change basis, so it
remains to be seen whether this moderation continues without further Fed tightening.
For now, the trends in the San Francisco Fed’s cyclical versus acyclical inflation measures
diverged again in August. As expected, given softening wage growth, prices most sensitive
to the labor market have eased further. At 3% YoY, this inflation category is down from
8% in early 2023 and 4.6% two years ago is back to prepandemic levels and is likely

to ease more (Exhibit 1A). In contrast, supply bottlenecks, high energy prices and other
industry-specific factors have kept acyclical inflation on an uptrend. As shown in Exhibit
1B, the relentless increase in freight costs could prolong upside pressure on this category.
The diesel market has tightened in recent months, boosting its price by 70% YoY,
consistent with Brent oil at around $200 per barrel and large reverberations across
a wide swath of goods and services prices. A government-policy-related decline in

available truck drivers is also raising transportation costs more than expected. So are
tight ocean shipping capacity and longer/more expensive voyages due to the U.S./Iran
War. Supply-chain stress also increased as companies rushed to front-run potential new
tariff increases.
On the positive side for the inflation outlook, unit labor costs (ULC) are up modestly
as robust productivity growth has absorbed much of the wage growth, limiting a major
source of domestic inflation. Upside productivity growth revisions are likely to further

reduce ULC, which could help shift focus from energy and AI-related inflation to
eventual disinflationary effects once oil and refined fuel prices start to ease. Sustained
productivity gains would support growth and margins while limiting inflation and the
need for much higher interest rates.
Investment Implications
We favor selective Equity exposure
to companies with durable demand
and pricing power, while remaining
cautious about businesses most

exposed to fuel, freight, and
financing costs. We would also
gradually increase long-duration
exposure.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
Surging nominal growth has boosted corporate revenues (up 13% YoY in Q2, according to the
BEA) while contained unit labor costs helped raise margins and profits more than expected.
With labor costs declining as a share of revenues, investment-related tax incentives and
rock-bottom net interest expense, pre-tax nonfinancial corporate profits as measured in GDP
accounts rose 21% YoY in Q2. Total corporate profits after-tax were up 18.2% YoY, according to

the BEA.
Surging AI-related imports and investment are keeping the business and profits cycle on
an uptrend both here and abroad. According to BofA Global Research, the global economic
cycle backdrop improved further in September, as reflected in a broad-based increase in its
Global Wave1 indicator. Broadening U.S. manufacturing strength, fueled by ongoing AI-driven
investment, a healthier orders backlog and low inventories suggest that the expansion has

room to run—a constructive environment for earnings. Typically, a Global Wave upswing is
consistent with rising earnings revisions ratios and equity price uptrends. According to the
Global Wave, global earnings growth expectations remain exceptionally strong.
Still, absent a resolution of war-related energy and transportation disruptions, the same
industrial strength intensifies inflation pressures. A prolonged supply shock raises the risk of
restrictive interest rates and weakening economic growth. In this context, the surge in the

10-year Treasury yield above 5% is starting to reflect more than optimism about U.S. growth
and strengthening productivity outlook. Oil’s tightest recent correlation with 10-year Treasury
yields in 35 years suggests a common energy-price thread behind rising yields in developed
countries.
Bond market spillovers from normalizing Japan yields and capital repatriation are another
common thread. Higher yields on foreign sovereign bonds such as in Japan—which has

escaped stagnation and deflation by intensely pursuing investment-led growth—weaken the
relative appeal of Treasurys. That distinction matters for portfolios. A yield increase driven
mainly by durable productivity gains would come with a stronger outlook for future income
and earnings. A yield increase driven by cost shocks and greater uncertainty raises financing
costs without the same benefit.
In sum, the economy remains more resilient than expected, but the bond market is also

charging more for an uncertain inflation outlook given untethered government debt and
cooling global demand for Treasurys spurred by geopolitical tensions and Japan capital
repatriation. The case for much further tightening becomes stronger if wage growth
strengthens, productivity lags, and unit labor costs reaccelerate, causing cyclical inflation to
also pick up steam. The case becomes weaker if that measure stays contained while fuel and
freight pressures recede or rising Treasury yields reduce asset prices and economic growth.

Exhibit 1: Softer Cyclical/Higher Acyclical Inflation Keeps “Core” PCE at 3% Year Through August.
A) Cooling wage pressures ease cyclical inflation (housing, food services and accommodation,
etc.).
B) Surge in U.S. AI-related imports, tariff front-running, shipping disruptions and fuel- price
spike feed acyclical inflation.

### Page 4

4 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
Yields, Yen and Iran: What Haunts the “House”
Lauren Sanfilippo, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
We recently highlighted the three-front challenge facing Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent:
engineering a stronger yen, containing the 10-year Treasury yield and securing a diplomatic
resolution to the conflict with Iran.

Bessent recently has delivered one of the year’s more memorable lines while discussing
Treasury policy and the yen: “I have asymmetric information. I am the house now.” 2 Yet several
challenges remain: the 10-year Treasury yield is near levels last seen in the early 2000s, the
yen remains structurally weaker, and a phased agreement to reopen the Strait of Hormuz
appears remote. The resulting strain on energy markets keeps inflation risks elevated.

Put differently, oil prices, Treasury yields and the dollar-yen exchange rate are all trending
against Bessent’s objectives. Below, we examine how each variable is haunting his “house.”
Haunting the “House” is the Yen. Since the U.S. and Japan mounted a record $98.7B
coordinated intervention in late July after the yen weakened to a 40-year low near 164 per
dollar, the currency has appreciated roughly 4% against the dollar. The move was further
supported on September 18 when the BoJ raised rates by 25 basis points (bps) to 1.25%,

their highest level since the mid-1990s (Exhibit 2A). However, the split 7–2 vote and Governor
Ueda’s limited forward guidance leave the path of further tightening uncertain. The key
question is whether additional BoJ hikes can produce sustained appreciation in the yen, or
merely reinforce the temporary relief provided by the summer intervention.
Bessent, meanwhile, has repeatedly signaled his preference for a stronger yen. Partly because
yen stability is also a U.S. financing concern. Persistent currency weakness could prompt

Japan to support its currency by selling foreign assets, including U.S. Treasurys—which would
be an unwelcome prospect as Washington partially relies on foreign capital flows to fund its
large fiscal deficits. That potential “haunts” the Treasury Secretary.  At precisely the moment
Washington needs foreign capital to finance its enormous fiscal deficits, it would be inopportune
for one of America’s largest creditors to become a significant seller of Treasury securities.

Haunting the “House” are Yields. The surge in yields runs counter to a White House intent
on lowering borrowing costs. The benchmark 10-year yield has broken above the 5% threshold
and climbed to levels not seen since 2007. At around 5.30%, it stands more than a full
percentage point above where it began the year (Exhibit 2B). The level is striking but so is the
speed of the move: Stronger-than-expected economic data, including a survey showing U.S.

2 September 8, 2026.
Investment Implications
While some key variables are
complicating the macro backdrop
(i.e., higher-for-longer yields/oil)
the combination of strong earnings
growth, robust consumer demand
(real final sales to private domestic

that the fundamental backdrop for

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
business activity expanded at the fastest pace in more than five years, helped trigger an 18
bps surge in the 10-year yield—among its largest daily moves in decades. The 5-year yield also
topped 5% for the first time since 2007, while the 30-year yield reached 5.63%, its highest
level since 2002.
Compounding the move is inflation, running above the Fed’s 2% target for 67 months,

large fiscal deficits and the prospect of continued growth in Treasury issuance. Against this
backdrop, Treasury bought back $4.1 billion of long-dated debt, versus a maximum of $6
billion. That effort has done little to arrest the selloff. A subsequent auction priced nearly $4
billion of 7-year notes at 5.085%, the highest yield for that maturity in 33 years.
All in, the MOVE index, a key gauge of Treasury market volatility, jumped from 78 to 95 on

Wednesday September 23 and then to 104 the following day, its highest level since the early
days of the Iran war (Exhibit 3A).
Haunting the “House” is the Iran conflict. Early expectations that military pressure and
economic sanctions would quickly bring Iran to the negotiating table have yet to materialize.
Iranian officials have signaled that a full reopening of the Strait of Hormuz remains unlikely
while sanctions stay in place. Although talks between Washington and Tehran are continuing,

conflicting signals and lingering skepticism from both sides have kept tensions elevated.
As a result, oil prices have remained elevated in a range even as some Middle East supply
disruptions began to ease.
Startling is that Brent crude is up more than 60% this year, but refined product prices
have risen even faster. Ongoing disruptions to energy flows through the Strait of Hormuz,
combined with Ukrainian strikes on Russian refining infrastructure have tightened global

supplies of crude oil and refined fuels. Saudi Arabia has restored some drone-disrupted flows,
providing roughly 3.5 million barrels a day of relief (roughly 3% of global consumption).
The squeeze is mostly acute in diesel markets, where inventories remain constrained and
prices continue to climb (Exhibit 3B). U.S. diesel prices recently surpassed $6.50 per gallon,
the highest level on record, just as Russia has extended restrictions on diesel exports into

October. Against this backdrop, proposals to ban U.S. fuel exports risks worsening the
imbalance. According to S&P Global, an export ban could reduce refinery runs by nearly 2
million barrels per day and significantly cut gasoline production, potentially driving gasoline
and jet fuel prices even higher. As long as tensions persist, the oil impulse remains a key
impediment to structurally lower inflation.
Investment implications. Treasury Secretary Bessent increasingly sits at the intersection of

monetary policy, fiscal policy, foreign policy and national security. We remain constructive on
the U.S. economy and overweight U.S. Equities but are watching as these fronts challenge the
narrative. There is a great deal riding on the “house” versus the key macro variables of today.

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
When It Comes to Attracting Foreign Capital, the U.S. Remains Exceptional
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
There may be no better vote of confidence in a country than the willingness of
foreigners to put their money there. And by that measurement, the U.S. remains the
exception—both portfolio and FDI inflows are currently running at record highs based

on the latest data. This dynamic is one key reason why we remain constructive on U.S.
assets over the longer term.
Notwithstanding all the handwringing about America’s fiscal deficits, tariffs and rising
government debt, global investors sunk some $942B in net purchases of U.S. Equities in
the 12 months to July, the highest rolling 12-month total on record. In Q2, net purchase
of U.S. Equities soared to $426B, up 62% from the same period a year ago (Exhibit 4A).

By country, flows were down from China—not surprisingly—but strong from Taiwan and
South Korea, who have taken to recycling their massive AI-generated trade surplus back
into the U.S. In the end, strong portfolio inflows suggest that international investors
remain willing to increase their exposure to the world's largest capital market—that’s
bullish for U.S. assets.
Meanwhile, Exhibit 4B highlights the growing push among foreign multinationals to be

inside the U.S., with FDI inflows totaling nearly $240B in the first six months of this year.
That is a staggering 103% jump from a year ago and means FDI inflows are on track for
a record year, estimated at $480B for 2026.
There are multiple factors behind surging inflows: One, due to rising U.S. trade
protectionism, foreign multinationals increasingly want to be “inside” the U.S. economy,
not outside. Two, within the $32 trillion-plus economy with extraordinarily consumer

purchasing power, foreign firms prefer to be closer to their customers via investment
than just trade (exports). Three, America’s massive investment cycle in AI, data centers,
semiconductors, power generation, etc., has created numerous potential investment
opportunities for many foreign firms that don’t want to miss out on America’s surging
capex cycle. Four, and finally, abundant energy supplies, deep capital markets and a highly
developed technological ecosystem have also induced more FDI inflows.

In the end, double-barreled capital inflows speak to the absolute and relative
attractiveness of the U.S. economy and capital markets. Stay invested in America—the
rest of the world is.

is bullish for U.S. securities, both near-

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

ICE BofA MOVE Index is a market-implied measure of U.S. bond market volatility.
Consumer Price Index measures the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a market basket of consumer goods and services.
Baltic Dry Index is a daily shipping and trade index published by the Baltic Exchange in London that measures the cost of moving raw materials like iron ore, coal, and grain by sea.
Volatility Index (VIX) is a real-time market indicator that measures the stock market's expected volatility over the next 30 days.

or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and

Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

## Document 309: Cliff Notes_ getting the balance right (2026-10-29)
